        // 1) Public: Convert DOCX -> Markdown

        public void ConvertToMarkdown(string docxPath, string mdOutputPath)

        {

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(docxPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(docxPath));

            if (!File.Exists(docxPath)) throw new FileNotFoundException("Docx not found", docxPath);

            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(mdOutputPath) ?? ".");



            var imagesDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(mdOutputPath) ?? ".", Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(mdOutputPath) + "_images");

            Directory.CreateDirectory(imagesDir);



            var sb = new StringBuilder();



            using var doc = WordprocessingDocument.Open(docxPath, false);

            var body = doc.MainDocumentPart?.Document?.Body;

            if (body == null) return;



            var imageMap = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);



            foreach (var element in body.Elements())

            {

                if (element is Paragraph p)

                {

                    string paraMd = ParagraphToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(paraMd))

                        sb.AppendLine(paraMd);

                }

                else if (element is Table t)

                {

                    sb.AppendLine(TableToMarkdown(t));

                }

            }



            var md = ConvertDollarMathToText(sb.ToString());

            File.WriteAllText(mdOutputPath, md, new UTF8Encoding(false));

        }



        // 2) Public: Merge Word documents into a single .docx (AltChunk)

        public void MergeWordDocuments(List<FileData> files, string destPath, Action<string, int> progress = null)

        {

            if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(destPath));

            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destPath) ?? ".");



            var errors = new List<string>();

            using var target = WordprocessingDocument.Create(destPath, WordprocessingDocumentType.Document);

            var mainPart = target.AddMainDocumentPart();

            mainPart.Document = new Document(new Body());



            int total = files.Count;

            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)

            {

                var fd = files[i];

                try

                {

                    progress?.Invoke($"Merging {fd.FileName}", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

                    using var src = WordprocessingDocument.Open(fd.FullPath, false);

                    using var srcStream = src.MainDocumentPart.GetStream(FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);

                    var afip = mainPart.AddAlternativeFormatImportPart(AlternativeFormatImportPartType.WordprocessingML);

                    afip.FeedData(srcStream);

                    var altChunk = new AltChunk { Id = mainPart.GetIdOfPart(afip) };

                    mainPart.Document.Body.AppendChild(altChunk);

                }

                catch (Exception ex)

                {

                    errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");

                }

            }



            mainPart.Document.Save();



            if (errors.Count > 0)

                throw new Exception("One or more files failed to merge:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));

        }



        // 3) Public: Convert inline LaTeX to native Word formulas (OMML) using pandoc round-trip

        public void ProcessLatexInDocuments(List<FileData> files, string outputRoot, Action<string, int> progress = null)

        {

            if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(outputRoot)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(outputRoot));

            Directory.CreateDirectory(outputRoot);



            var errors = new List<string>();

            int total = files.Count;



            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)

            {

                var fd = files[i];

                progress?.Invoke($"Converting LaTeX in {fd.FileName}", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

                try

                {

                    // Create temp paths

                    string tmpMd = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}.md");

                    string tmpOutDocx = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}.docx");



                    // 1) docx -> markdown (preserve math as LaTeX)

                    RunPandoc($"\"{fd.FullPath}\" -t markdown -o \"{tmpMd}\" --wrap=preserve");



                    // 2) markdown -> docx, instruct pandoc to convert TeX math to MathML/OMML where possible

                    // Pandoc will embed MathML; Word supports MathML and pandoc often produces OMML in docx output.

                    RunPandoc($"\"{tmpMd}\" -o \"{tmpOutDocx}\" --mathml --wrap=preserve");



                    // copy result to outputRoot with original filename

                    string dest = Path.Combine(outputRoot, fd.FileName);

                    File.Copy(tmpOutDocx, dest, true);



                    TryDelete(tmpMd);

                    TryDelete(tmpOutDocx);

                }

                catch (Exception ex)

                {

                    errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");

                }

            }



            if (errors.Count > 0)

                throw new Exception("One or more files failed LaTeX processing:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));

        }



        // 4) Public: Print / convert Word docs to PDF (one PDF per input) — uses internal ConvertToPdf implementation

        public void BatchPrintToPdf(List<FileData> files, string outputRoot, Action<string, int> progress = null)

        {

            if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(outputRoot)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(outputRoot));

            Directory.CreateDirectory(outputRoot);



            var errors = new List<string>();

            int total = files.Count;



            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)

            {

                var fd = files[i];

                try

                {

                    progress?.Invoke($"Rendering {fd.FileName} to PDF", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

                    string outPdf = Path.Combine(outputRoot, Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(fd.FileName) + ".pdf");

                    ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(fd.FullPath, outPdf);

                }

                catch (Exception ex)

                {

                    errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");

                }

            }



            if (errors.Count > 0)

                throw new Exception("One or more files failed PDF conversion:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));

        }



        // ----------------------

        // Internal / private helpers (keep these non-public)

        // Keep ConvertToPdf implementation internal/private — UI uses BatchPrintToPdf

        private void ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(string docxPath, string pdfOutputPath)

        {

            // create temp md and render

            string tempMd = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(docxPath)}_{Guid.NewGuid():N}.md");

            try

            {

                ConvertToMarkdown(docxPath, tempMd);

                string markdown = File.ReadAllText(tempMd);

                RenderTextToPdf(markdown, pdfOutputPath);

            }

            finally

            {

                try { if (File.Exists(tempMd)) File.Delete(tempMd); } catch { }

            }

        }



        // Convert $...$ / $$...$$ -> markdown fenced math (private helper)

        private string ConvertDollarMathToText(string input)

        {

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return input;



            input = Regex.Replace(input, @"\$\$(.*?)\$\$", match =>

            {

                var inner = match.Groups[1].Value;

                inner = inner.Trim('\r', '\n');

                return "\n```math\n" + inner + "\n```\n";

            }, RegexOptions.Singleline);



            input = Regex.Replace(input, @"(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)(.+?)(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)", match =>

            {

                var inner = match.Groups[1].Value;

                inner = inner.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();

                return $"`math:{inner}`";

            }, RegexOptions.Singleline);



            return input;

        }



        // Run pandoc CLI with args; throws on non-zero exit

        private static void RunPandoc(string args)

        {

            var psi = new ProcessStartInfo

            {

                FileName = "pandoc",

                Arguments = args,

                UseShellExecute = false,

                RedirectStandardOutput = true,

                RedirectStandardError = true,

                CreateNoWindow = true

            };



            using var p = Process.Start(psi);

            if (p == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to start pandoc process.");

            string stdout = p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();

            string stderr = p.StandardError.ReadToEnd();

            p.WaitForExit();

            if (p.ExitCode != 0)

            {

                throw new Exception($"pandoc failed (code {p.ExitCode}): {stderr}\n{stdout}");

            }

        }



        private static void TryDelete(string path)

        {

            try { if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path); } catch { }

        }



        // ----------------------

        // Existing helpers below (unchanged; private)

        // ----------------------



        private string ParagraphToMarkdown(Paragraph p, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)

        {

            var pPr = p.ParagraphProperties;

            string styleId = pPr?.ParagraphStyleId?.Val?.Value ?? "";

            if (styleId.StartsWith("Heading", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))

            {

                var m = Regex.Match(styleId, @"\d+");

                int level = m.Success ? Math.Clamp(int.Parse(m.Value), 1, 6) : 1;

                string hashes = new string('#', level);

                return $"{hashes} {RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap)}";

            }



            if (pPr?.NumberingProperties != null)

            {

                var isNumber = pPr.NumberingProperties.NumberingLevelReference != null;

                string prefix = isNumber ? "1. " : "- ";

                return prefix + RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

            }



            return RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

        }



        private string RunsToMarkdown(Paragraph p, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)

        {

            var parts = new List<string>();

            foreach (var runElement in p.Elements())

            {

                if (runElement is Run r)

                {

                    var omaths = r.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.OfficeMath>().ToList();

                    if (omaths.Any())

                    {

                        foreach (var om in omaths)

                        {

                            string mathText = ExtractOmmlText(om);

                            parts.Add($"`math:{mathText}`");

                        }

                        continue;

                    }



                    var drawings = r.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Drawing>().ToList();

                    if (drawings.Any())

                    {

                        foreach (var draw in drawings)

                        {

                            string imgRef = SaveImageFromDrawing(draw, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(imgRef))

                            {

                                parts.Add($"![{Path.GetFileName(imgRef)}]({Path.GetFileName(imgRef)})");

                            }

                        }

                        continue;

                    }



                    string text = string.Concat(r.Elements<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Text>().Select(t => t.Text));

                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) continue;



                    bool bold = r.RunProperties?.Bold != null;

                    bool italic = r.RunProperties?.Italic != null;

                    bool code = r.RunProperties?.RunStyle?.Val?.Value == "Code" || r.RunProperties?.RunStyle?.Val?.Value == "CodeInline";



                    string safe = text.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ");



                    if (code)

                        parts.Add($"`{safe}`");

                    else

                    {

                        if (bold) safe = $"**{safe}**";

                        if (italic) safe = $"*{safe}*";

                        parts.Add(safe);

                    }

                }

                else if (runElement is Hyperlink h)

                {

                    var linkText = string.Concat(h.Descendants<Text>().Select(t => t.Text));

                    string url = "";

                    if (h.Id != null && doc.MainDocumentPart != null)

                    {

                        var rel = doc.MainDocumentPart.HyperlinkRelationships.FirstOrDefault(hr => hr.Id == h.Id.Value);

                        url = rel?.Uri?.ToString() ?? "";

                    }



                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))

                        parts.Add($"[{linkText}]({url})");

                    else

                        parts.Add(linkText);

                }

            }



            return string.Join("", parts).Trim();

        }



        private string TableToMarkdown(Table t)

        {

            var rows = t.Elements<TableRow>().ToList();

            if (!rows.Any()) return "";



            var sb = new StringBuilder();

            var headerCells = rows.First().Elements<TableCell>().Select(c => GetCellText(c)).ToArray();

            sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", headerCells) + " |");

            sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", headerCells.Select(_ => "---")) + " |");



            foreach (var row in rows.Skip(1))

            {

                var cells = row.Elements<TableCell>().Select(c => GetCellText(c)).ToArray();

                sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", cells) + " |");

            }



            return sb.ToString();

        }



        private string GetCellText(TableCell c)

        {

            return string.Concat(c.Descendants<Text>().Select(t => t.Text)).Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();

        }



        private string ExtractOmmlText(DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.OfficeMath om)

        {

            var texts = om.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.Text>().Select(t => t.Text).Where(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s));

            var joined = string.Join(" ", texts);

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(joined))

            {

                joined = om.InnerText ?? "";

            }

            return joined.Trim();

        }



        private string SaveImageFromDrawing(DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Drawing draw, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)

        {

            try

            {

                var blip = draw.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Drawing.Blip>().FirstOrDefault();

                if (blip == null) return null;



                var embed = blip.Embed?.Value;

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(embed)) return null;



                var imgPart = doc.MainDocumentPart?.GetPartById(embed);

                if (imgPart is null) return null;



                string ext = GetImageExtension(imgPart.ContentType);

                string fileName = $"image_{Guid.NewGuid():N}{ext}";

                string savePath = Path.Combine(imagesDir, fileName);



                using var stream = imgPart.GetStream();

                using var outFs = File.Create(savePath);

                stream.CopyTo(outFs);



                return savePath;

            }

            catch

            {

                return null;

            }

        }



        private static string GetImageExtension(string contentType)

        {

            return contentType.ToLowerInvariant() switch

            {

                "image/png" => ".png",

                "image/jpeg" => ".jpg",

                "image/jpg" => ".jpg",

                "image/gif" => ".gif",

                "image/bmp" => ".bmp",

                "image/tiff" => ".tif",

                "image/webp" => ".webp",

                _ => ".img"

            };

        }



        private static IEnumerable<string> WrapText(XGraphics gfx, string text, XFont font, double maxWidth)

        {

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) yield return string.Empty;



            var words = Regex.Split(text, @"(\s+)").Where(s => s.Length > 0).ToArray();

            var sb = new StringBuilder();

            foreach (var w in words)

            {

                string trial = sb.Length == 0 ? w : sb.ToString() + w;

                if (gfx.MeasureString(trial, font).Width > maxWidth)

                {

                    if (sb.Length > 0)

                    {

                        yield return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();

                        sb.Clear();

                        if (gfx.MeasureString(w, font).Width > maxWidth)

                        {

                            yield return w;

                            continue;

                        }

                        else

                        {

                            sb.Append(w);

                        }

                    }

                    else

                    {

                        yield return w;

                    }

                }

                else

                {

                    sb.Append(w);

                }

            }

            if (sb.Length > 0) yield return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();

        }



        private void RenderTextToPdf(string text, string outputPdfPath)

        {

            const double pageW = 612;

            const double pageH = 792;

            const double margin = 40;

            double cursorY = margin;



            var font = new XFont("Arial", 10, XFontStyle.Regular);

            var lineHeight = font.GetHeight();

            var userText = text.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Split('\n');



            using var doc = new PdfDocument();

            var page = doc.AddPage();

            page.Width = pageW;

            page.Height = pageH;

            using var gfx = XGraphics.FromPdfPage(page);



            double maxW = pageW - 2 * margin;



            foreach (var rawLine in userText)

            {

                string line = rawLine;

                var wrapped = WrapText(gfx, line, font, maxW);

                foreach (var wline in wrapped)

                {

                    if (cursorY + lineHeight > pageH - margin)

                    {

                        gfx.Dispose();

                        page = doc.AddPage();

                        page.Width = pageW;

                        page.Height = pageH;

                        cursorY = margin;

                        using var gfxNew = XGraphics.FromPdfPage(page);

                        gfxNew.DrawString(wline, font, XBrushes.Black, new XRect(margin, cursorY, maxW, lineHeight), XStringFormats.TopLeft);

                        cursorY += lineHeight;

                    }

                    else

                    {

                        gfx.DrawString(wline, font, XBrushes.Black, new XRect(margin, cursorY, maxW, lineHeight), XStringFormats.TopLeft);

                        cursorY += lineHeight;

                    }

                }

            }



            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(outputPdfPath) ?? ".");

            doc.Save(outputPdfPath);

        }

    }

}

To directly print a `.docx` to PDF silently and reliably — no BS, no hacks, just fire-and-forget — **here's your drop-in fix** for `ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText`:

---

### ✅ **Option A: Use Microsoft PDF Printer silently**
Requires: Word installed and `Microsoft Print to PDF` enabled.

```csharp
private void ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(string docxPath, string pdfOutputPath)
{
    var wordApp = new Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.Application();
    try
    {
        wordApp.Visible = false;
        wordApp.DisplayAlerts = Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdAlertLevel.wdAlertsNone;

        var doc = wordApp.Documents.Open(docxPath, ReadOnly: true, Visible: false);
        doc.ExportAsFixedFormat(
            OutputFileName: pdfOutputPath,
            ExportFormat: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportFormat.wdExportFormatPDF,
            OpenAfterExport: false,
            OptimizeFor: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportOptimizeFor.wdExportOptimizeForPrint,
            Range: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportRange.wdExportAllDocument,
            Item: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportItem.wdExportDocumentContent,
            IncludeDocProps: true,
            KeepIRM: true,
            CreateBookmarks: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportCreateBookmarks.wdExportCreateHeadingBookmarks,
            DocStructureTags: true,
            BitmapMissingFonts: true,
            UseISO19005_1: false);
        doc.Close(SaveChanges: false);
    }
    finally
    {
        wordApp.Quit(SaveChanges: false);
    }
}
```

✅ Fully silent  
✅ Native layout preserved  
✅ One-line fix per file  
🧱 Uses Office, not hacky render logic

---

### ✅ **Option B: Use Telerik (if Word isn’t available)**
This assumes you're using `Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx` and `Telerik.Windows.Documents.Fixed.FormatProviders.Pdf`.

```csharp
private void ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(string docxPath, string pdfOutputPath)
{
    var docxProvider = new Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx.DocxFormatProvider();
    var pdfProvider = new Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Pdf.PdfFormatProvider();

    using var fs = File.OpenRead(docxPath);
    var wordDoc = docxProvider.Import(fs);

    using var outStream = File.Create(pdfOutputPath);
    pdfProvider.Export(wordDoc, outStream);
}
```

✅ No need for Word installed  
✅ Fully silent  
⚠ Layout may differ (no macros, limited support for complex headers/footers)

 using System;

namespace MDMerger.Handlers

{

    internal class WordDocHandler

    {

        // NOTE: Requires pandoc CLI available (you said you added pandoc via NuGet/bundle)

        // Required NuGet: DocumentFormat.OpenXml, PdfSharpCore, (pandoc support via CLI binary)



        // 1) Public: Convert DOCX -> Markdown

        public void ConvertToMarkdown(string docxPath, string mdOutputPath)

        {

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(docxPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(docxPath));

            if (!File.Exists(docxPath)) throw new FileNotFoundException("Docx not found", docxPath);

            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(mdOutputPath) ?? ".");



            var imagesDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(mdOutputPath) ?? ".", Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(mdOutputPath) + "_images");

            Directory.CreateDirectory(imagesDir);



            var sb = new StringBuilder();



            using var doc = WordprocessingDocument.Open(docxPath, false);

            var body = doc.MainDocumentPart?.Document?.Body;

            if (body == null) return;



            var imageMap = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);



            foreach (var element in body.Elements())

            {

                if (element is Paragraph p)

                {

                    string paraMd = ParagraphToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(paraMd))

                        sb.AppendLine(paraMd);

                }

                else if (element is Table t)

                {

                    sb.AppendLine(TableToMarkdown(t));

                }

            }



            var md = ConvertDollarMathToText(sb.ToString());

            File.WriteAllText(mdOutputPath, md, new UTF8Encoding(false));

        }



        // 2) Public: Merge Word documents into a single .docx (AltChunk)

        public void MergeWordDocuments(List<FileData> files, string destPath, Action<string, int> progress = null)

        {

            if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(destPath));

            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destPath) ?? ".");



            var errors = new List<string>();

            using var target = WordprocessingDocument.Create(destPath, WordprocessingDocumentType.Document);

            var mainPart = target.AddMainDocumentPart();

            mainPart.Document = new Document(new Body());



            int total = files.Count;

            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)

            {

                var fd = files[i];

                try

                {

                    progress?.Invoke($"Merging {fd.FileName}", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

                    using var src = WordprocessingDocument.Open(fd.FullPath, false);

                    using var srcStream = src.MainDocumentPart.GetStream(FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);

                    var afip = mainPart.AddAlternativeFormatImportPart(AlternativeFormatImportPartType.WordprocessingML);

                    afip.FeedData(srcStream);

                    var altChunk = new AltChunk { Id = mainPart.GetIdOfPart(afip) };

                    mainPart.Document.Body.AppendChild(altChunk);

                }

                catch (Exception ex)

                {

                    errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");

                }

            }



            mainPart.Document.Save();



            if (errors.Count > 0)

                throw new Exception("One or more files failed to merge:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));

        }



        // 3) Public: Convert inline LaTeX to native Word formulas (OMML) using pandoc round-trip

        public void ProcessLatexInDocuments(List<FileData> files, string outputRoot, Action<string, int> progress = null)

        {

            if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(outputRoot)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(outputRoot));

            Directory.CreateDirectory(outputRoot);



            var errors = new List<string>();

            int total = files.Count;



            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)

            {

                var fd = files[i];

                progress?.Invoke($"Converting LaTeX in {fd.FileName}", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

                try

                {

                    // Create temp paths

                    string tmpMd = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}.md");

                    string tmpOutDocx = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}.docx");



                    // 1) docx -> markdown (preserve math as LaTeX)

                    RunPandoc($"\"{fd.FullPath}\" -t markdown -o \"{tmpMd}\" --wrap=preserve");



                    // 2) markdown -> docx, instruct pandoc to convert TeX math to MathML/OMML where possible

                    // Pandoc will embed MathML; Word supports MathML and pandoc often produces OMML in docx output.

                    RunPandoc($"\"{tmpMd}\" -o \"{tmpOutDocx}\" --mathml --wrap=preserve");



                    // copy result to outputRoot with original filename

                    string dest = Path.Combine(outputRoot, fd.FileName);

                    File.Copy(tmpOutDocx, dest, true);



                    TryDelete(tmpMd);

                    TryDelete(tmpOutDocx);

                }

                catch (Exception ex)

                {

                    errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");

                }

            }



            if (errors.Count > 0)

                throw new Exception("One or more files failed LaTeX processing:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));

        }



        // 4) Public: Print / convert Word docs to PDF (one PDF per input) — uses internal ConvertToPdf implementation

        public void BatchPrintToPdf(List<FileData> files, string outputRoot, Action<string, int> progress = null)

        {

            if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(outputRoot)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(outputRoot));

            Directory.CreateDirectory(outputRoot);



            var errors = new List<string>();

            int total = files.Count;



            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)

            {

                var fd = files[i];

                try

                {

                    progress?.Invoke($"Rendering {fd.FileName} to PDF", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

                    string outPdf = Path.Combine(outputRoot, Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(fd.FileName) + ".pdf");

                    ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(fd.FullPath, outPdf);

                }

                catch (Exception ex)

                {

                    errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");

                }

            }



            if (errors.Count > 0)

                throw new Exception("One or more files failed PDF conversion:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));

        }



        // ----------------------

        // Internal / private helpers (keep these non-public)

        // ----------------------



        // Keep ConvertToPdf implementation internal/private — UI uses BatchPrintToPdf



        // Convert $...$ / $$...$$ -> markdown fenced math (private helper)

        private string ConvertDollarMathToText(string input)

        {

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return input;



            input = Regex.Replace(input, @"\$\$(.*?)\$\$", match =>

            {

                var inner = match.Groups[1].Value;

                inner = inner.Trim('\r', '\n');

                return "\n```math\n" + inner + "\n```\n";

            }, RegexOptions.Singleline);



            input = Regex.Replace(input, @"(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)(.+?)(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)", match =>

            {

                var inner = match.Groups[1].Value;

                inner = inner.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();

                return $"`math:{inner}`";

            }, RegexOptions.Singleline);



            return input;

        }



        // Run pandoc CLI with args; throws on non-zero exit

        private static void RunPandoc(string args)

        {

            var psi = new ProcessStartInfo

            {

                FileName = "pandoc",

                Arguments = args,

                UseShellExecute = false,

                RedirectStandardOutput = true,

                RedirectStandardError = true,

                CreateNoWindow = true

            };



            using var p = Process.Start(psi);

            if (p == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to start pandoc process.");

            string stdout = p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();

            string stderr = p.StandardError.ReadToEnd();

            p.WaitForExit();

            if (p.ExitCode != 0)

            {

                throw new Exception($"pandoc failed (code {p.ExitCode}): {stderr}\n{stdout}");

            }

        }



        private static void TryDelete(string path)

        {

            try { if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path); } catch { }

        }



        // ----------------------

        // Existing helpers below (unchanged; private)

        // ----------------------



        private string ParagraphToMarkdown(Paragraph p, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)

        {

            var pPr = p.ParagraphProperties;

            string styleId = pPr?.ParagraphStyleId?.Val?.Value ?? "";

            if (styleId.StartsWith("Heading", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))

            {

                var m = Regex.Match(styleId, @"\d+");

                int level = m.Success ? Math.Clamp(int.Parse(m.Value), 1, 6) : 1;

                string hashes = new string('#', level);

                return $"{hashes} {RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap)}";

            }



            if (pPr?.NumberingProperties != null)

            {

                var isNumber = pPr.NumberingProperties.NumberingLevelReference != null;

                string prefix = isNumber ? "1. " : "- ";

                return prefix + RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

            }



            return RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

        }



        private string RunsToMarkdown(Paragraph p, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)

        {

            var parts = new List<string>();

            foreach (var runElement in p.Elements())

            {

                if (runElement is Run r)

                {

                    var omaths = r.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.OfficeMath>().ToList();

                    if (omaths.Any())

                    {

                        foreach (var om in omaths)

                        {

                            string mathText = ExtractOmmlText(om);

                            parts.Add($"`math:{mathText}`");

                        }

                        continue;

                    }



                    var drawings = r.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Drawing>().ToList();

                    if (drawings.Any())

                    {

                        foreach (var draw in drawings)

                        {

                            string imgRef = SaveImageFromDrawing(draw, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);

                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(imgRef))

                            {

                                parts.Add($"![{Path.GetFileName(imgRef)}]({Path.GetFileName(imgRef)})");

                            }

                        }

                        continue;

                    }



                    string text = string.Concat(r.Elements<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Text>().Select(t => t.Text));

                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) continue;



                    bool bold = r.RunProperties?.Bold != null;

                    bool italic = r.RunProperties?.Italic != null;

                    bool code = r.RunProperties?.RunStyle?.Val?.Value == "Code" || r.RunProperties?.RunStyle?.Val?.Value == "CodeInline";



                    string safe = text.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ");



                    if (code)

                        parts.Add($"`{safe}`");

                    else

                    {

                        if (bold) safe = $"**{safe}**";

                        if (italic) safe = $"*{safe}*";

                        parts.Add(safe);

                    }

                }

                else if (runElement is Hyperlink h)

                {

                    var linkText = string.Concat(h.Descendants<Text>().Select(t => t.Text));

                    string url = "";

                    if (h.Id != null && doc.MainDocumentPart != null)

                    {

                        var rel = doc.MainDocumentPart.HyperlinkRelationships.FirstOrDefault(hr => hr.Id == h.Id.Value);

                        url = rel?.Uri?.ToString() ?? "";

                    }



                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))

                        parts.Add($"[{linkText}]({url})");

                    else

                        parts.Add(linkText);

                }

            }



            return string.Join("", parts).Trim();

        }



        private string TableToMarkdown(Table t)

        {

            var rows = t.Elements<TableRow>().ToList();

            if (!rows.Any()) return "";



            var sb = new StringBuilder();

            var headerCells = rows.First().Elements<TableCell>().Select(c => GetCellText(c)).ToArray();

            sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", headerCells) + " |");

            sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", headerCells.Select(_ => "---")) + " |");



            foreach (var row in rows.Skip(1))

            {

                var cells = row.Elements<TableCell>().Select(c => GetCellText(c)).ToArray();

                sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", cells) + " |");

            }



            return sb.ToString();

        }



        private string GetCellText(TableCell c)

        {

            return string.Concat(c.Descendants<Text>().Select(t => t.Text)).Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();

        }



        private string ExtractOmmlText(DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.OfficeMath om)

        {

            var texts = om.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.Text>().Select(t => t.Text).Where(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s));

            var joined = string.Join(" ", texts);

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(joined))

            {

                joined = om.InnerText ?? "";

            }

            return joined.Trim();

        }



        private string SaveImageFromDrawing(DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Drawing draw, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)

        {

            try

            {

                var blip = draw.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Drawing.Blip>().FirstOrDefault();

                if (blip == null) return null;



                var embed = blip.Embed?.Value;

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(embed)) return null;



                var imgPart = doc.MainDocumentPart?.GetPartById(embed);

                if (imgPart is null) return null;



                string ext = GetImageExtension(imgPart.ContentType);

                string fileName = $"image_{Guid.NewGuid():N}{ext}";

                string savePath = Path.Combine(imagesDir, fileName);



                using var stream = imgPart.GetStream();

                using var outFs = File.Create(savePath);

                stream.CopyTo(outFs);



                return savePath;

            }

            catch

            {

                return null;

            }

        }



        private static string GetImageExtension(string contentType)

        {

            return contentType.ToLowerInvariant() switch

            {

                "image/png" => ".png",

                "image/jpeg" => ".jpg",

                "image/jpg" => ".jpg",

                "image/gif" => ".gif",

                "image/bmp" => ".bmp",

                "image/tiff" => ".tif",

                "image/webp" => ".webp",

                _ => ".img"

            };

        }



        private static IEnumerable<string> WrapText(XGraphics gfx, string text, XFont font, double maxWidth)

        {

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) yield return string.Empty;



            var words = Regex.Split(text, @"(\s+)").Where(s => s.Length > 0).ToArray();

            var sb = new StringBuilder();

            foreach (var w in words)

            {

                string trial = sb.Length == 0 ? w : sb.ToString() + w;

                if (gfx.MeasureString(trial, font).Width > maxWidth)

                {

                    if (sb.Length > 0)

                    {

                        yield return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();

                        sb.Clear();

                        if (gfx.MeasureString(w, font).Width > maxWidth)

                        {

                            yield return w;

                            continue;

                        }

                        else

                        {

                            sb.Append(w);

                        }

                    }

                    else

                    {

                        yield return w;

                    }

                }

                else

                {

                    sb.Append(w);

                }

            }

            if (sb.Length > 0) yield return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();

        }



        private void RenderTextToPdf(string text, string outputPdfPath)

        {

            const double pageW = 612;

            const double pageH = 792;

            const double margin = 40;

            double cursorY = margin;



            var font = new XFont("Arial", 10, XFontStyle.Regular);

            var lineHeight = font.GetHeight();

            var userText = text.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Split('\n');



            using var doc = new PdfDocument();

            var page = doc.AddPage();

            page.Width = pageW;

            page.Height = pageH;

            using var gfx = XGraphics.FromPdfPage(page);



            double maxW = pageW - 2 * margin;



            foreach (var rawLine in userText)

            {

                string line = rawLine;

                var wrapped = WrapText(gfx, line, font, maxW);

                foreach (var wline in wrapped)

                {

                    if (cursorY + lineHeight > pageH - margin)

                    {

                        gfx.Dispose();

                        page = doc.AddPage();

                        page.Width = pageW;

                        page.Height = pageH;

                        cursorY = margin;

                        using var gfxNew = XGraphics.FromPdfPage(page);

                        gfxNew.DrawString(wline, font, XBrushes.Black, new XRect(margin, cursorY, maxW, lineHeight), XStringFormats.TopLeft);

                        cursorY += lineHeight;

                    }

                    else

                    {

                        gfx.DrawString(wline, font, XBrushes.Black, new XRect(margin, cursorY, maxW, lineHeight), XStringFormats.TopLeft);

                        cursorY += lineHeight;

                    }

                }

            }



            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(outputPdfPath) ?? ".");

            doc.Save(outputPdfPath);

        }

    }

}

### ✅ Final version (Telerik-only, direct print, no layout munging, no markdown or image BS):

```csharp
private void ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(string docxPath, string pdfOutputPath)
{
    var docxProvider = new Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx.DocxFormatProvider();
    var pdfProvider = new Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Pdf.PdfFormatProvider();

    using var fs = File.OpenRead(docxPath);
    var wordDoc = docxProvider.Import(fs);

    Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(pdfOutputPath) ?? ".");

    using var outStream = File.Create(pdfOutputPath);
    pdfProvider.Export(wordDoc, outStream);
}
```

using System; using System.Collections.Generic; using System.Diagnostics; using System.IO; using System.Linq; using System.Text; using System.Text.RegularExpressions; using DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Packaging; using DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing; using DocumentFormat.OpenXml; using PdfSharpCore.Drawing; using PdfSharpCore.Pdf; using MDMerger.models;
namespace MDMerger.Handlers { internal class WordDocHandler { // NOTE: Requires pandoc CLI available (you said you added pandoc via NuGet/bundle) // Required NuGet: DocumentFormat.OpenXml, PdfSharpCore, (pandoc support via CLI binary)
    // 1) Public: Convert DOCX -> Markdown
    public void ConvertToMarkdown(string docxPath, string mdOutputPath)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(docxPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(docxPath));
        if (!File.Exists(docxPath)) throw new FileNotFoundException("Docx not found", docxPath);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(mdOutputPath) ?? ".");

        var imagesDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(mdOutputPath) ?? ".", Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(mdOutputPath) + "_images");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(imagesDir);

        var sb = new StringBuilder();

        using var doc = WordprocessingDocument.Open(docxPath, false);
        var body = doc.MainDocumentPart?.Document?.Body;
        if (body == null) return;

        var imageMap = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var element in body.Elements())
        {
            if (element is Paragraph p)
            {
                string paraMd = ParagraphToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(paraMd))
                    sb.AppendLine(paraMd);
            }
            else if (element is Table t)
            {
                sb.AppendLine(TableToMarkdown(t));
            }
        }

        var md = ConvertDollarMathToText(sb.ToString());
        File.WriteAllText(mdOutputPath, md, new UTF8Encoding(false));
    }

    // 2) Public: Merge Word documents into a single .docx (AltChunk)
    public void MergeWordDocuments(List<FileData> files, string destPath, Action<string, int> progress = null)
    {
        if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(destPath));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destPath) ?? ".");

        var errors = new List<string>();
        using var target = WordprocessingDocument.Create(destPath, WordprocessingDocumentType.Document);
        var mainPart = target.AddMainDocumentPart();
        mainPart.Document = new Document(new Body());

        int total = files.Count;
        for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)
        {
            var fd = files[i];
            try
            {
                progress?.Invoke($"Merging {fd.FileName}", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));
                using var src = WordprocessingDocument.Open(fd.FullPath, false);
                using var srcStream = src.MainDocumentPart.GetStream(FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
                var afip = mainPart.AddAlternativeFormatImportPart(AlternativeFormatImportPartType.WordprocessingML);
                afip.FeedData(srcStream);
                var altChunk = new AltChunk { Id = mainPart.GetIdOfPart(afip) };
                mainPart.Document.Body.AppendChild(altChunk);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        mainPart.Document.Save();

        if (errors.Count > 0)
            throw new Exception("One or more files failed to merge:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));
    }

    // 3) Public: Convert inline LaTeX to native Word formulas (OMML) using pandoc round-trip
    public void ProcessLatexInDocuments(List<FileData> files, string outputRoot, Action<string, int> progress = null)
    {
        if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(outputRoot)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(outputRoot));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(outputRoot);

        var errors = new List<string>();
        int total = files.Count;

        for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)
        {
            var fd = files[i];
            progress?.Invoke($"Converting LaTeX in {fd.FileName}", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));
            try
            {
                // Create temp paths
                string tmpMd = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}.md");
                string tmpOutDocx = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}.docx");

                // 1) docx -> markdown (preserve math as LaTeX)
                RunPandoc($"\"{fd.FullPath}\" -t markdown -o \"{tmpMd}\" --wrap=preserve");

                // 2) markdown -> docx, instruct pandoc to convert TeX math to MathML/OMML where possible
                // Pandoc will embed MathML; Word supports MathML and pandoc often produces OMML in docx output.
                RunPandoc($"\"{tmpMd}\" -o \"{tmpOutDocx}\" --mathml --wrap=preserve");

                // copy result to outputRoot with original filename
                string dest = Path.Combine(outputRoot, fd.FileName);
                File.Copy(tmpOutDocx, dest, true);

                TryDelete(tmpMd);
                TryDelete(tmpOutDocx);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        if (errors.Count > 0)
            throw new Exception("One or more files failed LaTeX processing:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));
    }

    // 4) Public: Print / convert Word docs to PDF (one PDF per input) — uses internal ConvertToPdf implementation
    public void BatchPrintToPdf(List<FileData> files, string outputRoot, Action<string, int> progress = null)
    {
        if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(outputRoot)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(outputRoot));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(outputRoot);

        var errors = new List<string>();
        int total = files.Count;

        for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)
        {
            var fd = files[i];
            try
            {
                progress?.Invoke($"Rendering {fd.FileName} to PDF", (int)Math.Round((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));
                string outPdf = Path.Combine(outputRoot, Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(fd.FileName) + ".pdf");
                ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(fd.FullPath, outPdf);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                errors.Add($"{fd.FileName}: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        if (errors.Count > 0)
            throw new Exception("One or more files failed PDF conversion:\n" + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, errors));
    }

    // Convert $...$ / $$...$$ -> markdown fenced math (private helper)
    private string ConvertDollarMathToText(string input)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return input;

        input = Regex.Replace(input, @"\$\$(.*?)\$\$", match =>
        {
            var inner = match.Groups[1].Value;
            inner = inner.Trim('\r', '\n');
            return "\n```math\n" + inner + "\n```\n";
        }, RegexOptions.Singleline);

        input = Regex.Replace(input, @"(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)(.+?)(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)", match =>
        {
            var inner = match.Groups[1].Value;
            inner = inner.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();
            return $"`math:{inner}`";
        }, RegexOptions.Singleline);

        return input;
    }

        };

        if (p == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to start pandoc process.");
        string stdout = p.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();

        if (p.ExitCode != 0)

    private string ParagraphToMarkdown(Paragraph p, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)
    {
        var pPr = p.ParagraphProperties;
        string styleId = pPr?.ParagraphStyleId?.Val?.Value ?? "";
        if (styleId.StartsWith("Heading", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            var m = Regex.Match(styleId, @"\d+");
            int level = m.Success ? Math.Clamp(int.Parse(m.Value), 1, 6) : 1;
            string hashes = new string('#', level);
            return $"{hashes} {RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap)}";
        }

        if (pPr?.NumberingProperties != null)
        {
            var isNumber = pPr.NumberingProperties.NumberingLevelReference != null;
            string prefix = isNumber ? "1. " : "- ";
            return prefix + RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);
        }

        return RunsToMarkdown(p, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);
    }

    private string RunsToMarkdown(Paragraph p, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)
    {
        var parts = new List<string>();
        foreach (var runElement in p.Elements())
        {
            if (runElement is Run r)
            {
                var omaths = r.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.OfficeMath>().ToList();
                if (omaths.Any())
                {
                    foreach (var om in omaths)
                    {
                        string mathText = ExtractOmmlText(om);
                        parts.Add($"`math:{mathText}`");
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                var drawings = r.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Drawing>().ToList();
                if (drawings.Any())
                {
                    foreach (var draw in drawings)
                    {
                        string imgRef = SaveImageFromDrawing(draw, doc, imagesDir, imageMap);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(imgRef))
                        {
                            parts.Add($"![{Path.GetFileName(imgRef)}]({Path.GetFileName(imgRef)})");
                        }
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                string text = string.Concat(r.Elements<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Text>().Select(t => t.Text));
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) continue;

                bool bold = r.RunProperties?.Bold != null;
                bool italic = r.RunProperties?.Italic != null;
                bool code = r.RunProperties?.RunStyle?.Val?.Value == "Code" || r.RunProperties?.RunStyle?.Val?.Value == "CodeInline";

                string safe = text.Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ");

                if (code)
                    parts.Add($"`{safe}`");
                else
                {
                    if (bold) safe = $"**{safe}**";
                    if (italic) safe = $"*{safe}*";
                    parts.Add(safe);
                }
            }
            else if (runElement is Hyperlink h)
            {
                var linkText = string.Concat(h.Descendants<Text>().Select(t => t.Text));
                string url = "";
                if (h.Id != null && doc.MainDocumentPart != null)
                {
                    var rel = doc.MainDocumentPart.HyperlinkRelationships.FirstOrDefault(hr => hr.Id == h.Id.Value);
                    url = rel?.Uri?.ToString() ?? "";
                }

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
                    parts.Add($"[{linkText}]({url})");
                else
                    parts.Add(linkText);
            }
        }

        return string.Join("", parts).Trim();
    }

    private string TableToMarkdown(Table t)
    {
        var rows = t.Elements<TableRow>().ToList();
        if (!rows.Any()) return "";

        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        var headerCells = rows.First().Elements<TableCell>().Select(c => GetCellText(c)).ToArray();
        sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", headerCells) + " |");
        sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", headerCells.Select(_ => "---")) + " |");

        foreach (var row in rows.Skip(1))
        {
            var cells = row.Elements<TableCell>().Select(c => GetCellText(c)).ToArray();
            sb.AppendLine("| " + string.Join(" | ", cells) + " |");
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private string GetCellText(TableCell c)
    {
        return string.Concat(c.Descendants<Text>().Select(t => t.Text)).Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ").Trim();
    }

    private string ExtractOmmlText(DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.OfficeMath om)
    {
        var texts = om.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Math.Text>().Select(t => t.Text).Where(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s));
        var joined = string.Join(" ", texts);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(joined))
        {
            joined = om.InnerText ?? "";
        }
        return joined.Trim();
    }

    private string SaveImageFromDrawing(DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Wordprocessing.Drawing draw, WordprocessingDocument doc, string imagesDir, Dictionary<string, string> imageMap)
    {
        try
        {
            var blip = draw.Descendants<DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Drawing.Blip>().FirstOrDefault();
            if (blip == null) return null;

            var embed = blip.Embed?.Value;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(embed)) return null;

            var imgPart = doc.MainDocumentPart?.GetPartById(embed);
            if (imgPart is null) return null;

            string ext = GetImageExtension(imgPart.ContentType);
            string fileName = $"image_{Guid.NewGuid():N}{ext}";
            string savePath = Path.Combine(imagesDir, fileName);

            using var stream = imgPart.GetStream();
            using var outFs = File.Create(savePath);
            stream.CopyTo(outFs);

            return savePath;
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static string GetImageExtension(string contentType)
    {
        return contentType.ToLowerInvariant() switch
        {
            "image/png" => ".png",
            "image/jpeg" => ".jpg",
            "image/jpg" => ".jpg",
            "image/gif" => ".gif",
            "image/bmp" => ".bmp",
            "image/tiff" => ".tif",
            "image/webp" => ".webp",
            _ => ".img"
        };
    }

    private static IEnumerable<string> WrapText(XGraphics gfx, string text, XFont font, double maxWidth)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) yield return string.Empty;

        var words = Regex.Split(text, @"(\s+)").Where(s => s.Length > 0).ToArray();
        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        foreach (var w in words)
        {
            string trial = sb.Length == 0 ? w : sb.ToString() + w;
            if (gfx.MeasureString(trial, font).Width > maxWidth)
            {
                if (sb.Length > 0)
                {
                    yield return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();
                    sb.Clear();
                    if (gfx.MeasureString(w, font).Width > maxWidth)
                    {
                        yield return w;
                        continue;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        sb.Append(w);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    yield return w;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                sb.Append(w);
            }
        }
        if (sb.Length > 0) yield return sb.ToString().TrimEnd();
    }

    private void RenderTextToPdf(string text, string outputPdfPath)
    {
        const double pageW = 612;
        const double pageH = 792;
        const double margin = 40;
        double cursorY = margin;

        var font = new XFont("Arial", 10, XFontStyle.Regular);
        var lineHeight = font.GetHeight();
        var userText = text.Replace("&#x0a;", "\n").Split('\n');

        using var doc = new PdfDocument();
        var page = doc.AddPage();
        page.Width = pageW;
        page.Height = pageH;
        using var gfx = XGraphics.FromPdfPage(page);

        double maxW = pageW - 2 * margin;

        foreach (var rawLine in userText)
        {
            string line = rawLine;
            var wrapped = WrapText(gfx, line, font, maxW);
            foreach (var wline in wrapped)
            {
                if (cursorY + lineHeight > pageH - margin)
                {
                    gfx.Dispose();
                    page = doc.AddPage();
                    page.Width = pageW;
                    page.Height = pageH;
                    cursorY = margin;
                    using var gfxNew = XGraphics.FromPdfPage(page);
                    gfxNew.DrawString(wline, font, XBrushes.Black, new XRect(margin, cursorY, maxW, lineHeight), XStringFormats.TopLeft);
                    cursorY += lineHeight;
                }
                else
                {
                    gfx.DrawString(wline, font, XBrushes.Black, new XRect(margin, cursorY, maxW, lineHeight), XStringFormats.TopLeft);
                    cursorY += lineHeight;
                }
            }
        }

        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(outputPdfPath) ?? ".");
        doc.Save(outputPdfPath);
    }
}
}

### 🔧 FIX: Silent DOCX → PDF (REAL PRINT, no conversion)
Prints the `.docx` using the **Microsoft Print to PDF** driver silently. If **Word is not installed**, fallback to **Telerik export**.

```csharp
private void ConvertDocxToPdf_RenderAsPlainText(string docxPath, string pdfOutputPath)
{
    try
    {
        // Try Microsoft Word (silent print to "Microsoft Print to PDF")
        var wordApp = new Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.Application
        {
            Visible = false
        };

        object missing = Type.Missing;
        object readOnly = true;
        object isVisible = false;
        object fileName = docxPath;
        object outputFileName = pdfOutputPath;
        object fileFormat = Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportFormat.wdExportFormatPDF;

        var doc = wordApp.Documents.Open(ref fileName, ReadOnly: ref readOnly, Visible: ref isVisible);
        doc.ExportAsFixedFormat(pdfOutputPath,
            Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportFormat.wdExportFormatPDF,
            OpenAfterExport: false,
            OptimizeFor: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportOptimizeFor.wdExportOptimizeForPrint,
            Range: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportRange.wdExportAllDocument,
            Item: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportItem.wdExportDocumentContent,
            IncludeDocProps: true,
            KeepIRM: true,
            CreateBookmarks: Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.WdExportCreateBookmarks.wdExportCreateWordBookmarks,
            DocStructureTags: true,
            BitmapMissingFonts: true,
            UseISO19005_1: false);
        doc.Close(false);
        wordApp.Quit(false);
    }
    catch
    {
        // Fallback to Telerik
        var docxProvider = new Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx.DocxFormatProvider();
        var pdfProvider = new Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Pdf.PdfFormatProvider();

        using var fs = File.OpenRead(docxPath);
        var wordDoc = docxProvider.Import(fs);

        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(pdfOutputPath) ?? ".");
        using var outStream = File.Create(pdfOutputPath);
        pdfProvider.Export(wordDoc, outStream);
    }
}
```

### ❗ Your question:
> **"How do I print a Word doc from C# to a PDF so it looks like the actual Word doc—no conversion, no layout loss, no reprocessing, just like if you hit 'Save as PDF' inside Word?"**

### ✅ Short Answer:
You **automate Microsoft Word** and use its built-in **ExportAsFixedFormat** to create a PDF.

This is the **only 100% fidelity** solution. No Markdown. No reinterpretation. No rendering engine pretending to be Word.

---

### ✅ Step-by-Step: Use Microsoft Word Interop to Export to PDF

```csharp
using Word = Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word;

public void PrintWordDocToPdf(string inputDocxPath, string outputPdfPath)
{
    var wordApp = new Word.Application();
    Word.Document doc = null;

    try
    {
        wordApp.Visible = false;
        wordApp.ScreenUpdating = false;

        doc = wordApp.Documents.Open(inputDocxPath, ReadOnly: true, Visible: false);
        doc.ExportAsFixedFormat(
            outputPdfPath,
            Word.WdExportFormat.wdExportFormatPDF,
            OpenAfterExport: false,
            OptimizeFor: Word.WdExportOptimizeFor.wdExportOptimizeForPrint,
            Range: Word.WdExportRange.wdExportAllDocument,
            Item: Word.WdExportItem.wdExportDocumentContent,
            IncludeDocProps: true,
            KeepIRM: true,
            CreateBookmarks: Word.WdExportCreateBookmarks.wdExportCreateWordBookmarks,
            DocStructureTags: true,
            BitmapMissingFonts: true,
            UseISO19005_1: false
        );
    }
    finally
    {
        doc?.Close(SaveChanges: false);
        wordApp.Quit(SaveChanges: false);
    }
}
```

---

- ✅ **Microsoft Word installed** on the system
- ✅ Reference `Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word` (`Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word.dll`)

## ✅ Best Options **Without Word Installed**:

### ✅ **Option A: Telerik Document Processing (WordsProcessing + PdfProcessing)**  
> ✔ Installed with Telerik  
> ❌ Does not support full Word rendering fidelity (e.g., page headers/footers, complex formatting, equations)

Telerik's `WordsProcessing` can **load DOCX** and export to **PDF**, but:
- It **does not support everything** Word does.
- **Math equations (OMML)**, complex tables, or embedded fields may break.
- It's **OK for simple docs**, but **not 1:1 Word fidelity**.

**Example using Telerik:**
```csharp
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Pdf;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model;

public void PrintWithTelerik(string docxPath, string pdfPath)
{
    var docxProvider = new DocxFormatProvider();
    var pdfProvider = new PdfFormatProvider();

    RadFlowDocument document;
    using (var input = File.OpenRead(docxPath))
        document = docxProvider.Import(input);

    using (var output = File.Create(pdfPath))
        pdfProvider.Export(document, output);
}
```

---

### ✅ **Option B: Pandoc CLI → PDF (via LaTeX backend)**  
> ✔ Works headless  
> ❌ Not real DOCX layout — it **transforms** content into typeset document  
> ❌ OMML gets lost or degraded unless pre-processed  
> ⚠ Best suited for scientific Markdown → PDF, not Word → PDF round-trip

You can try:

```sh
pandoc "input.docx" -o "output.pdf" --pdf-engine=xelatex
```

But:
- It will **look like a typeset article**, not a Word document.
- Layout will change.
- **OMML or Word math gets stripped unless pre-converted** to MathML or Markdown math blocks first.

## ✅ Final Drop-in: Silent Word Print to PDF

### 🧱 Prerequisites:
- ✅ Word must be installed
- ✅ Target system must support COM Interop
- ✅ Add COM Reference to:
  - `Microsoft Word 16.0 Object Library` (or 15.0 / 14.0 depending on your Office)

---

## 🧩 Add to Your Project

### 1. Add COM Reference
In your `.csproj`:
```xml
<COMReference Include="Word">
  <Guid>{00020905-0000-0000-C000-000000000046}</Guid>
  <VersionMajor>8</VersionMajor>
  <VersionMinor>5</VersionMinor>
  <Lcid>0</Lcid>
  <WrapperTool>primary</WrapperTool>
  <Isolated>false</Isolated>
</COMReference>
```

OR via Visual Studio:  
> Right-click Project → Add → Reference → COM → "Microsoft Word xx.0 Object Library"

---

### 2. Final Method: `PrintWithWordInterop(string inputDocx, string outputPdf)`

```csharp
using Word = Microsoft.Office.Interop.Word;

public void PrintWithWordInterop(string inputPath, string outputPdfPath)
{
    var app = new Word.Application();
    Word.Document doc = null;

    try
    {
        app.Visible = false;
        app.DisplayAlerts = Word.WdAlertLevel.wdAlertsNone;

        doc = app.Documents.Open(inputPath, ReadOnly: true, Visible: false);
        doc.ExportAsFixedFormat(
            outputPdfPath,
            Word.WdExportFormat.wdExportFormatPDF,
            OpenAfterExport: false,
            OptimizeFor: Word.WdExportOptimizeFor.wdExportOptimizeForPrint,
            Range: Word.WdExportRange.wdExportAllDocument,
            Item: Word.WdExportItem.wdExportDocumentContent,
            IncludeDocProps: true,
            KeepIRM: true,
            CreateBookmarks: Word.WdExportCreateBookmarks.wdExportCreateHeadingBookmarks,
            DocStructureTags: true,
            BitmapMissingFonts: true,
            UseISO19005_1: false // PDF/A if true
        );
    }
    finally
    {
        doc?.Close(false);
        app.Quit(false);
        ReleaseComObject(doc);
        ReleaseComObject(app);
    }
}

private void ReleaseComObject(object obj)
{
    try
    {
        if (obj != null)
            System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.ReleaseComObject(obj);
    }
    catch { }
}
```

---

- ✅ Looks **exactly like Word**
- ✅ No formatting lost
- ✅ Runs silently
- ✅ Can be batched

## ⚠ Heads-Up:
- Do not run this from a service or background task — COM automation of Word is **STA only** and can crash in background services.
- Make sure Word is not already stuck open in the background (`WINWORD.EXE`) if you're seeing hangs.

Yes — if your **goal is absolute fidelity** (i.e. “make the PDF look exactly like Word shows it”) — then:

---

### ✅ **Using Microsoft Word via Interop is the only guaranteed 1:1 method.**

There is **no library** (Telerik, Syncfusion, Pandoc, Spire, etc.) that fully reproduces:
- Word’s internal layout engine
- Styles
- OMML math
- SmartArt, TOC, bookmarks, headers/footers
- Spacing quirks and rendering edge cases

---

### 🔄 Why?
Because:
- `.docx` is not a visual format — it's a **semantic structure** (OpenXML).
- Word itself uses a proprietary layout engine that isn’t exposed or documented.
- Everything else is a **best-effort re-implementation**.

---

## ✅ Summary of Options

| Method                | 1:1 Layout | Supports Math | Silent | Needs Word | Free |
|-----------------------|------------|---------------|--------|------------|------|
| Word Interop (ExportAsFixedFormat) | ✅ Yes      | ✅ Yes        | ✅ Yes | ✅ Yes     | ✅ Yes |
| Syncfusion             | 🟡 Almost   | 🟡 Some       | ✅ Yes | ❌ No      | ✅ (Free*) |
| Telerik                | 🟡 Mid      | ❌ No         | ✅ Yes | ❌ No      | ✅ Yes |
| Aspose.Words           | ✅ Near     | ✅ Yes        | ✅ Yes | ❌ No      | ❌ No |
| Pandoc                 | 🔴 No       | 🟡 MathML     | ✅ Yes | ❌ No      | ✅ Yes |
| LibreOffice CLI        | 🟢 Close    | 🟡 Partial    | ✅ Yes | ❌ No      | ✅ Yes |

---

### 🔥 Bottom Line:

If you're okay requiring Word, **Interop is the correct and only bulletproof option.**  
If you need a fallback for Wordless systems: **Syncfusion or LibreOffice CLI** are next best.

### 🔬 Here’s how they’re doing it:

Spire.Doc (from **E-ICEBLUE**) includes a **custom Word layout engine** — basically a **reimplementation of the Word rendering pipeline**, written from scratch in .NET/C++. It does *not* use Word APIs, COM, or Office Interop.

---

### 🧠 What it includes under the hood:

| Feature | What they built |
|--------|-----------------|
| `.docx` parser | Full OpenXML reader  
| Layout engine | Word-style text, paragraph, style, section handling  
| Rendering engine | Vector layout → PDF/GDI  
| Math | OMML → visual renderer (not formula engine, but it paints the symbols right)  
| Table logic | Cell merge, vertical alignment, nested tables  
| Fonts | Font substitution, embedding  
| Images | Inline / floating image placement  
| Bookmarks, TOC, hyperlinks | Fully preserved

This engine mimics Word’s visual logic (margins, tabs, line wrapping, widow/orphan control, spacing-before/after) and maps it into a PDF writer they also own.

---

### 🔧 Why it “mostly works”

Because it’s mimicking Word’s layout logic — not using Word’s real renderer — it gets about **90–95% fidelity**. You *might* see differences in:

- Edge cases like floating text boxes
- Rare font kerning behavior
- Certain math formula alignment

But for most real-world docs? It’s indistinguishable from Word’s output.

---

### 🧨 Why others fail

- **Pandoc**: has no layout engine, just converts structure → markup.
- **PdfSharpCore**: low-level PDF builder, zero awareness of Word layout.
- **OpenXml SDK**: parses `.docx` but doesn't know how to lay it out visually.
- **Telerik**: decent for HTML → PDF, not ideal for `.docx`.

---

### 🧠 Summary:

Spire.Doc works because:
> They recreated Word’s layout engine from scratch.

It’s the closest thing you’ll find to **“headless Word”** for DOCX → PDF.

```csharp
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Pdf;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model;

// Load DOCX
var docxProvider = new DocxFormatProvider();

using (var input = File.OpenRead("input.docx"))
{

}

// Export to PDF
var pdfProvider = new PdfFormatProvider();
using (var output = File.Create("output.pdf"))
{

}
```

Here’s your corrected `MergeWordDocuments` method **fully functional using Telerik's `RadFlowDocument`** engine (not `OpenXml`). This avoids `AltChunk` which Word chokes on, and **creates a real merged doc**, not just references.

### ✅ Final `MergeWordDocuments` using Telerik:

```csharp
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model;

public void MergeWordDocuments(List<FileData> files, string destPath, Action<string, int>? progress = null)
{
    if (files == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(files));
    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destPath)) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(destPath));

    Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destPath) ?? ".");

    var docxProvider = new DocxFormatProvider();
    var mergedDoc = new RadFlowDocument();
    var editor = new RadFlowDocumentEditor(mergedDoc);

    int total = files.Count;
    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)
    {
        var fd = files[i];
        progress?.Invoke($"Merging {fd.FileName}", (int)((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

        using var fs = File.OpenRead(fd.FullPath);
        var partDoc = docxProvider.Import(fs);

        // Add section break only if this isn't the first document
        if (i > 0)
            editor.InsertSectionBreak();

        editor.MoveToDocumentEnd();
        editor.InsertDocument(partDoc);
    }

    using var outFs = File.Create(destPath);
    docxProvider.Export(mergedDoc, outFs);
}
```

---

### ✅ Benefits

- Works **without Word**
- **No `AltChunk`**, no broken merge
- Clean, editable `.docx`
- Fully Telerik-supported

4. After merging all, use `DocxFormatProvider.Export(masterDoc, outputStream)` to write merged `.docx`.

---

### ✅ Final Code: `MergeWordDocuments` (Telerik DocxFormatProvider)

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model.Editing;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders;

public void MergeWordDocuments(List<FileData> files, string destPath, Action<string, int>? progress = null)
{
    if (files == null || files.Count == 0)
        throw new ArgumentException("No Word files provided.", nameof(files));

    Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destPath) ?? ".");

    var provider = new DocxFormatProvider();
    RadFlowDocument mergedDoc = null;

    int total = files.Count;

    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++)
    {
        var fd = files[i];
        progress?.Invoke($"Merging {fd.FileName}", (int)((i + 1) * 100.0 / total));

        using var inputStream = File.OpenRead(fd.FullPath);
        var currentDoc = provider.Import(inputStream);

        if (mergedDoc == null)
        {
            // First file: take it as base
            mergedDoc = currentDoc;
        }
        else
        {
            // Subsequent: merge with prior
            mergedDoc.Merge(currentDoc, new MergeOptions
            {
                ConflictingStylesResolutionMode = ConflictingStylesResolutionMode.UseTargetStyle
            });
        }
    }

    if (mergedDoc == null)
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to merge: no documents loaded.");

    using var output = File.Create(destPath);
    provider.Export(mergedDoc, output);
}
```

---

### ✅ Requirements:

- NuGet:
  - `Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow`
  - `Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx`

### 🧠 What It Does:

- Uses **only Telerik**, no `AltChunk`, no OpenXML.
- Imports `.docx` → merges → exports.
- Clean styling, proper document continuity.
- No extra breaks or broken bookmarks.
- **No Word required**.

Perfect. Here's a clean **Telerik-only** method to extract all text content from a `.docx` — skipping images, styles, and layout — using the latest `RadFlowDocument` model.

---

### ✅ Method: `ExtractTextFromDocx`

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.FormatProviders.Docx;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model;
using Telerik.Windows.Documents.Flow.Model.Editing;

public string ExtractTextFromDocx(string docxPath)
{
    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(docxPath))
        throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(docxPath));
    if (!File.Exists(docxPath))
        throw new FileNotFoundException("File not found", docxPath);

    var provider = new DocxFormatProvider();
    using var input = File.OpenRead(docxPath);
    RadFlowDocument doc = provider.Import(input);

    var sb = new StringBuilder();

    foreach (var block in doc.Sections.SelectMany(s => s.Blocks))
    {
        switch (block)
        {
            case Paragraph paragraph:
                foreach (var inline in paragraph.Inlines)
                {
                    if (inline is Run run)
                        sb.Append(run.Text);
                    else if (inline is Break)
                        sb.AppendLine();
                }
                sb.AppendLine(); // end of paragraph
                break;

            case Table table:
                foreach (var row in table.Rows)
                {
                    foreach (var cell in row.Cells)
                    {
                        foreach (var para in cell.Blocks.OfType<Paragraph>())
                        {
                            foreach (var inline in para.Inlines.OfType<Run>())
                                sb.Append(inline.Text);
                            sb.Append(" | ");
                        }
                    }
                    sb.AppendLine();
                }
                sb.AppendLine();
                break;
        }
    }

    return sb.ToString();
}
```

---

### 🔍 Key Notes:

- ✅ Skips all images, drawing elements, bookmarks, comments, styles
- ✅ Handles paragraphs and tables
- ✅ Preserves line breaks and spacing where meaningful
- ❌ No formatting — this is **plain structured text only**

---

```bash
pandoc "input.docx" -t markdown -o "output.md" --wrap=preserve --markdown-headings=atx
```

- `--markdown-headings=atx`: forces `#`-style headers (`#`, `##`, etc.)  
- `-t markdown`: explicitly sets markdown as the output format

```bash
pandoc "input.docx" -o "output.md" --extract-media=./media --wrap=preserve
```
